● Write(docs\advisory-pipeline\01-architecture.md)
  ⎿  Added 108 lines, removed 55 lines
        1  # Advisory Pipeline — Architecture
        2  
        3 -> Dora's travel advisory system proactively recommends places,
          - warns about scams, and suggests activities to users during th
          -eir trips by scraping Reddit, TripAdvisor, and Google Maps, pr
          -ocessing results through LLMs, and delivering structured advis
          -ories.                                                        
        3 +> Dora's travel advisory system proactively recommends POIs, w
          +arns about scams, and suggests activities to travelers during 
          +live trips. It scrapes Reddit + Google Maps, ranks results via
          + rules + LLM, and delivers push notifications — all driven by 
          +a per-trip "brain" that tracks state, adapts to user feedback,
          + and pauses on inactivity.                                    
        4  
        5  ## System Overview
        6  
        7  ```
        8  Flutter App
        9      │
       10 -    ├── POST /advisory/start        (pre-trip or location trig
          -ger)                                                          
       11 -    ├── POST /advisory/query        (natural-language: "find m
          -e a quiet cafe")                                              
       12 -    ├── GET  /advisory/insights     (inbox: pending + delivere
          -d advisories)                                                 
       10 +    ├── POST /advisory/start        (pre-trip or manual reseed
          +)                                                             
       11 +    ├── POST /advisory/query        (NL: "find me a quiet cafe
          +")                                                            
       12 +    ├── POST /advisory/pause        (manual pipeline pause)   
       13 +    ├── POST /advisory/resume       (explicit resume)         
       14 +    ├── GET  /advisory/insights     (inbox: pending + delivere
          +d)                                                            
       15 +    ├── GET  /advisory/state        (debug: brain state, gated
          +)                                                             
       16      └── POST /advisory/{id}/action  (liked / dismissed / saved
            / acted_on)
       17            │
       18            ▼
       16 -    FastAPI Backend (Railway)                                 
       19 +    FastAPI Backend                                           
       20            │
       18 -          ├── Advisory Service (creates job, records actions) 
       21 +          ├── Advisory Service (creates jobs, records actions)
       22 +          ├── Trip Brain Service (per-trip state machine)     
       23            └── advisory_jobs table (status=queued)
       24                  │
       25                  ▼
       22 -    Advisory Worker (Railway Worker Service, polls PostgreSQL)
       26 +    ┌─── Advisory Cycle Worker (polls trip_advisory_state ever
          +y 30s) ──┐                                                    
       27 +    │                                                         
          +          │                                                   
       28 +    │  For each active trip whose next_eligible_at <= now():  
          +          │                                                   
       29 +    │    pick_next_target(trip_id)                            
          +          │                                                   
       30 +    │      route mode: sample polyline, project GPS, geocode n
          +ext city │                                                    
       31 +    │      radius mode: centroid of live GPS, reverse geocode 
          +         │                                                    
       32 +    │    → create location_trigger AdvisoryJob                
          +         │                                                    
       33 +    └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
          +─────────┘                                                    
       34 +                │                                             
       35 +                ▼                                             
       36 +    Advisory Worker (polls advisory_jobs)                     
       37            │
       24 -          ├── 1. route_segmentation   → segment trip into citi
          -es, derive keywords                                           
       25 -          ├── 2. reddit_scrape        → Crawl4AI deep crawl + 
          -LLM extraction                                                
       26 -          ├── 3. tripadvisor_scrape   → Crawl4AI (stub, future
          -)                                                             
       27 -          ├── 4. gmaps_scrape         → Bright Data CDP (stub,
          - future)

       58  
       59  ## Data Flow
       60  
       39 -### Pre-Trip (background, at trip creation)                   
       40 -1. User creates trip with metadata (traveler_type, travel_styl
          -e, activity_focus)                                            
       41 -2. Flutter calls `POST /trips/{id}/advisory/start` with `job_t
          -ype=pre_trip`                                                 
       42 -3. API creates `advisory_jobs` row with `status=queued`       
       43 -4. Worker picks up job, reads trip places, builds search keywo
          -rds                                                           
       44 -5. Crawl4AI deep-crawls Reddit (BestFirst strategy + keyword s
          -coring)                                                       
       45 -6. LLM extracts structured insights from each discovered post 
       46 -7. Insights are deduped, scored, and written as `trip_advisori
          -es` rows                                                      
       47 -8. Low-confidence advisories go to inbox; high-confidence also
          - get push notification                                        
       61 +### Trip Creation (auto-seed)                                 
       62 +1. User creates trip → `trip_advisory_state` row auto-created 
          +(lifecycle=seeded)                                            
       63 +2. Trip classified by rule table (long_road / short_road / int
          +ra_city / day_trip / multi_day_leisure)                       
       64 +3. If route exists: polyline sampled into 22 equal-distance po
          +ints, each reverse-geocoded (Mapbox)                          
       65 +4. Metadata snapshot frozen (trip_metadata + user_metadata)   
       66  
       49 -### On-Demand (user asks during trip)                         
       50 -1. User responds to notification or types "find me breakfast s
          -pots"                                                         
       51 -2. Flutter calls `POST /trips/{id}/advisory/query` with raw te
          -xt                                                            
       52 -3. API stores `query_text` and creates `on_demand` job (no LLM
          - in request path)                                             
       53 -4. Worker parses intent in `route_segmentation` stage, then sc
          -rapes accordingly                                             
       54 -5. Same pipeline: scrape → extract → dedupe → score → deliver 
       67 +### Tracking Start (enrich + activate)                        
       68 +1. User starts live tracking → brain enriched (weather for fir
          +st samples)                                                   
       69 +2. lifecycle_state → active, next_eligible_at set             
       70 +3. Cycle worker begins firing every `cadence_seconds` (determi
          +ned by trip class)                                            
       71  
       56 -### Location Trigger (approaching a city)                     
       57 -1. Live tracking worker detects user approaching a new city/se
          -gment                                                         
       58 -2. Backend calls advisory service to create `location_trigger`
          - job                                                          
       59 -3. If an active location_trigger job already exists for this t
          -rip, the new trigger payload is coalesced into the existing jo
          -b's `scrape_plan` (row-locked transaction)                    
       60 -4. Worker processes the coalesced triggers                    
       72 +### Per-Cycle Advisory (location_trigger)                     
       73 +1. Cycle worker picks next uncovered locality (route mode) or 
          +geocodes live centroid (radius mode)                          
       74 +2. Creates location_trigger AdvisoryJob with target context   
       75 +3. Worker runs: Reddit scrape for target locality → GMaps POI 
          +search → rule-filter (weather/dedupe/warnings) → soft-score (d
          +ietary/time/activity) → LLM picks top 3 with copy             
       76 +4. TripAdvisory rows persisted with poi_place_id + weather_sna
          +pshot                                                         
       77 +5. Firebase push for confidence ≥ 0.7 (Redis per-user throttle
          +: max 3/hr)                                                   
       78 +6. Brain updated: locality + POIs recorded, categories balance
          +d, next_eligible_at advanced                                  
       79  
       80 +### User Feedback Loop                                        
       81 +- **Accept** (liked/saved/acted_on/converted_to_place): ignore
          +_streak reset → 0; if paused due to inactivity, implicit resum
          +e                                                             
       82 +- **Reject** (dismissed): ignore_streak reset → 0; no re-sugge
          +stion of that POI                                             
       83 +- **Ignore** (no action for 1hr): sweep marks expired, brain.i
          +gnore_streak++                                                
       84 +- **3 consecutive ignores**: pipeline paused, "paused due to i
          +nactivity" push sent                                          
       85 +- **Manual pause**: `POST /advisory/pause` → paused_reason='us
          +er'; only explicit `POST /advisory/resume` clears             
       86 +                                                              
       87 +### Route/Metadata Change (reseed)                            
       88 +1. Route update detected via `routes.geom_sig` change → reseed
          +(route_changed)                                               
       89 +2. Trip metadata update → reseed(metadata_changed)            
       90 +3. Debounced: if last_seed_at < 120s ago, reasons queued in pe
          +nding_reseed_reasons[]                                        
       91 +4. Cycle worker flushes pending reseeds after window; single c
          +oalesced run                                                  
       92 +5. Preserves advised_locality_keys + advised_poi_place_ids + i
          +gnore_streak                                                  
       93 +                                                              
       94 +### Pre-Trip / On-Demand (manual)                             
       95 +- `POST /advisory/start` with `job_type=pre_trip` → force rese
          +ed or one-shot insights                                       
       96 +- `POST /advisory/query` with NL text → on_demand job, worker 
          +parses intent                                                 
       97 +                                                              
       98 +## Trip Classification Rule Table                             
       99 +                                                              
      100 +| trip_class | Detection | cadence |                          
      101 +|---|---|---|                                                 
      102 +| long_road | route exists, distance > 300km | 60min |        
      103 +| short_road | route exists, 50 < distance ≤ 300km | 30min |  
      104 +| multi_day_leisure | duration > 3d, distance ≤ 300km | 120min
          + |                                                            
      105 +| day_trip | duration ≤ 1d, distance ≤ 150km | 45min |        
      106 +| intra_city | no route OR distance < 50km | 90min |          
      107 +| unclassified | fallback | 60min |                           
      108 +                                                              
      109 +Intra-city uses **radius mode** (rolling 2h GPS centroid); all
          + others use **route mode** (polyline sample traversal).       
      110 +                                                              
      111  ## Key Design Decisions
      112  
      113  | Decision | Rationale |
      114  |----------|-----------|
       66 -| NL intent parsing in worker, not API | API returns 202 insta
          -ntly; no LLM latency or failure risk in request path |        
       67 -| Advisory status = delivery-only (pending/delivered/expired) 
          -| User engagement is append-only in `advisory_user_actions` — 
          -preserves full action history |                               
       68 -| Job idempotency via `(trip_id, job_type, request_hash)` part
          -ial unique index | Prevents duplicate active jobs while allowi
          -ng multiple distinct on_demand queries |                      
       69 -| `request_hash` is NOT NULL with canonical normalization | Av
          -oids PostgreSQL NULL-skips-unique-index gotcha |              
       70 -| Stale threshold = 900s (15 min) + heartbeat every 60s | Redd
          -it/LLM stages can take 5-10 min; prevents false stale recovery
          - |                                                            
       71 -| Push throttle: max 3/hour per trip | Prevents notification f
          -atigue; checked via DB count query in delivery stage |        
       72 -| Confidence gating: >= 0.7 for push, all >= 0.3 for inbox | L
          -ow-confidence still visible in inbox, just no push interruptio
          -n |                                                           
       73 -| Upstash Redis for ephemeral session state, Supabase DB for p
          -ersistent data | Per-trip cache expires; user actions and advi
          -sories persist for learning |                                 
      115 +| Per-trip durable brain (`trip_advisory_state`) + Redis hot c
          +ache | Auditable state machine; Redis only for hot reads + cen
          +troid buffer |                                                
      116 +| Claim-then-process cycle worker with make_interval lease | S
          +hort row-lock on claim (ms); crashed worker's claim auto-expir
          +es |                                                          
      117 +| mark_cycle_outcome with 5 outcomes (delivered/no_pick/failed
          +/no_target/off_route) | Eliminates stall where next_eligible_a
          +t never advances |                                            
      118 +| no_pick retry budget (2) before locality marked covered | Tr
          +ansient failures (API blip, thin scrape results) don't permane
          +ntly kill a locality |                                        
      119 +| Off-route skip (5km nearest-leg threshold via route_geom geo
          +graphy) | Prevents advising on wrong corridor when user deviat
          +es |                                                          
      120 +| Atomic BrightData counter with cap check in single UPDATE | 
          +No double-spend under concurrent cycle ticks or retries |     
      121 +| Push throttle per-user (not per-trip) via Redis INCR | Stric
          +ter across multi-trip scenarios; avoids notification spam |   
      122 +| Ignore detection via TTL sweep + NOT EXISTS guarded UPDATE |
          + Race-safe with late user actions; defense-in-depth in apply_f
          +eedback |                                                     
      123 +| Reseed debounce (120s) with pending_reseed_reasons TEXT[] | 
          +Prevents thrashing on rapid route edits; single coalesced run 
          +|

● Good. I've absorbed both the Codex findings and my own exploration. Here's my
  honest read across all three workstreams, then I want your reaction before any
   planning.

  ---
  1. Flutter Advisory Wiring — bigger than it looks

  Advisory is zero in Flutter right now. Not "partially wired" — zero:
  - No generated client methods (OpenAPI spec doesn't include /advisory/ at all)
  - No manual bridge code (the 1069-line live_tracking_api.dart has 24 methods,
  none advisory)
  - No screens, no widgets, no providers, no notification routing for advisory
  type
  - No feature flag for advisory subsystem
  - No user_metadata model or UI

  The existing notification handler (live_tracking_deep_link_bootstrap.dart)
  blindly assumes all push payloads are trip-related — it extracts trip_id and
  routes based on session state. Our advisory pushes send type: "advisory" and
  action: "open_inbox" but nothing reads those fields.

  My take: This isn't a "wire some API calls" task. It's a new feature vertical
  in Flutter: data layer (API client + repository + providers), presentation
  layer (inbox screen + detail sheet + action buttons), and integration layer
  (notification routing + feature gate). The advisory inbox is conceptually
  similar to the existing checkin candidate inbox strip but much richer.

  2. OpenAPI Regeneration — cascading issue

  The generated client (flutter/packages/dora_api/) uses dart-dio from
  openapi-generator-config.yaml. It covers 19 live-tracking methods but is
  stale:
  - Missing: all advisory endpoints (7), user_metadata CRUD (2), /advisory/state
   debug endpoint
  - Missing: any V2 endpoints that were added after the last generation
  - The live_tracking_api.dart manual bridge exists specifically because the
  generated client fell behind

  Two paths forward:
  - (a) Regenerate properly: Export fresh openapi.json from the FastAPI backend
  (/openapi.json endpoint), run the generator, get a clean client with all
  endpoints. Then retire manual duplicates from live_tracking_api.dart.
  - (b) Extend the manual bridge only: Add advisory methods to
  live_tracking_api.dart by hand. Faster but keeps the generated/manual split
  messier.

  I lean (a) — it's a one-time cleanup that pays for itself, and FastAPI
  auto-generates the OpenAPI spec.

  3. UI/UX — the real conversation

  From the code exploration, here's what users actually experience today:

  Editor screen (the good):
  - Map works: pan, zoom, tap-to-add places, markers + route lines rendered
  - Timeline sidebar (desktop) / DraggableScrollableSheet (mobile) with
  reorderable places + routes
  - Place/route tap → bottom detail panel expands (AnimatedContainer)
  - Design tokens consistently applied

  Editor screen (the dev-lean):
  - Detail panel is 284px fixed height, non-scrollable — forms overflow and get
  clipped
  - Route lines clickable but no visual hint they're tappable (no hover/press
  state)
  - Timeline items draggable but no drag handle indicator shown to user
  - No confirmed-action feedback (add place → no toast, delete → no
  confirmation)
  - V1 code paths still present behind flags, creating conditional rendering
  noise

  Live capture screen (the good):
  - Full-screen map with location marker
  - Action dock (Photo/Note/Warn/Media/Tag) with staggered entry animation
  - Start/Pause/Resume/Stop controls work
  - Recent events strip at bottom (tappable event bubbles)

  Live capture screen (the dev-lean):
  - No bottom sheet at all — everything stacked as overlays on the map
  - Events are small bubbles, not expandable — can't see details without
  navigating away
  - No scrollable timeline of captured events
  - Checkin candidates show in a banner strip, not integrated into a unified
  sheet

  ---
  The Google Maps mental model you want:

  Google Maps packs search, directions, place details, reviews, photos, nearby,
  and street view into one screen using a single draggable bottom sheet with 3
  snap points (peek → half → full). Everything happens in the sheet; the map
  shrinks/expands in response.

  For Dora, that would mean:

  - Map always fills the screen (both live and editor)
  - One unified DraggableScrollableSheet with snap points (peek: 15%, half: 50%,
   full: 85%)
  - Sheet has tabs/segments: Timeline | Advisories | Current Detail
  - Tapping a place/event on map → sheet auto-slides to that item's detail card
  (scrollIntoView)
  - Advisory insights appear as cards in the Advisories tab, tappable for POI
  detail
  - Accept/reject/ignore actions are inline buttons on each advisory card
  - Live capture actions (Photo/Note/Warn) float as FABs above the sheet, not a
  separate dock
  - Same sheet pattern on both editor and live screen — user learns one
  interaction model

  The sheet content would be:
  - Timeline tab: scrollable, reorderable list of places/routes/events (merges
  editor sidebar + live recent-events into one pattern)
  - Advisories tab: advisory inbox cards (pulled from GET /advisory/insights),
  each with accept/dismiss/save buttons
  - Detail tab: when an item is selected, full detail form (place edit, route
  edit, advisory POI detail) fills the sheet

  This is a significant UI rework but the foundation is there —
  DraggableScrollableSheet is already used in the editor's mobile layout. The
  work is: (a) unify it across both screens, (b) add the advisory tab, (c) make
  everything scrollable/tappable/animated.

  ---
  What I want to hear from you before planning:

  1. Do you agree with the "one unified sheet" model, or do you see the live
  screen and editor screen as fundamentally different interaction patterns?
  2. For advisory wiring: path (a) OpenAPI regen or path (b) extend manual
  bridge?
  3. Priority order: do we wire advisory first (backend → Flutter data layer →
  minimal UI) and then polish UI/UX? Or redesign the UI/UX first (the sheet/tabs
   pattern) and then fill it with advisory content?

     Plan: OpenAPI Regen + Flutter Advisory Wiring

     Context

     The advisory pipeline backend is complete (trip brain, cycle engine, POI
     selector, feedback loop — 40 tests pass, migration applied). But Flutter
     has zero advisory code: no generated client methods, no manual bridge, no
     screens, no providers, no notification routing. The OpenAPI-generated
     client (flutter/packages/dora_api/) was last regenerated April 1st and is
     missing all advisory + recent V2 endpoints.

     Goal of this slice: regenerate the OpenAPI client, wire advisory data layer
      in Flutter, add a minimal test UI to exercise the full backend round-trip
     (create trip → start advisory → see insights → accept/dismiss → observe
     brain state). Full UI/UX rework is deferred.

     ---
     Step A: OpenAPI Client Regeneration

     Status: Fresh openapi.json already exported to flutter/openapi.json (208KB,
      8 advisory paths confirmed with Advisory tag).

     Actions

     1. Run the generator:
     cd flutter
     npx @openapitools/openapi-generator-cli generate -c
     openapi-generator-config.yaml
     2. Verify output:
       - packages/dora_api/lib/src/api/advisory_api.dart exists with methods for
      all 8 endpoints
       - packages/dora_api/lib/src/model/ has advisory request/response models
       - packages/dora_api/lib/src/api.dart exports the new AdvisoryApi class
       - Run dart analyze packages/dora_api/ — zero errors
     3. Fix any generation issues:
       - dart-dio + built_value sometimes chokes on complex JSONB fields — if
     models fail to build, add manual @BuiltValueSerializer overrides or
     simplify the backend schema types
       - If Advisory tag produces a class name collision, rename in
     openapi-generator-config.yaml via importMapping
     4. Verify existing API classes still compile:
       - flutter pub get in both packages/dora_api/ and the main app
       - dart analyze on the full flutter project
     5. Commit the regenerated client + fresh openapi.json

     Files touched

     - flutter/openapi.json (replaced with fresh export)
     - flutter/packages/dora_api/lib/src/api/advisory_api.dart (new, generated)
     - flutter/packages/dora_api/lib/src/model/advisory_*.dart (new, generated)
     - Various existing generated files may get minor updates (new fields,
     reordered imports)

     ---
     Step B: Flutter Advisory Data Layer

     B1. Advisory Repository

     flutter/lib/features/advisory/data/advisory_repository.dart

     Wraps the generated AdvisoryApi with typed, error-handled methods. Pattern
     mirrors existing repositories.

     Critical: The AdvisoryApi instance MUST be constructed with the same
     authenticated Dio client + interceptors that LiveTrackingApi uses. Check
     how live_tracking_api.dart creates its Dio instance (likely from a shared
     DioFactory or provider that injects the Supabase JWT bearer token). The
     AdvisoryRepository constructor takes that same Dio, constructs
     AdvisoryApi(dio: dio), and all calls inherit auth + retry + base-URL config
      automatically. No separate Dio stack.

     class AdvisoryRepository {
       final AdvisoryApi _api;

       // Constructed with shared authenticated Dio from the app's DI
       AdvisoryRepository(Dio authenticatedDio)
         : _api = AdvisoryApi(authenticatedDio);

       Future<AdvisoryJobResponse> startAdvisory(String tripId, {String jobType
     = 'pre_trip'});
       Future<AdvisoryJobResponse> queryAdvisory(String tripId, String
     queryText);
       Future<List<AdvisoryInsightResponse>> listInsights(String tripId,
     {String? category, int page = 1});
       Future<List<AdvisoryJobResponse>> listJobs(String tripId, {String?
     status, int page = 1});
       Future<AdvisoryActionResponse> recordAction(String advisoryId, String
     action, {Map<String, dynamic>? metadata});
       Future<void> pauseAdvisory(String tripId);
       Future<void> resumeAdvisory(String tripId);
       Future<AdvisoryBrainState> getAdvisoryState(String tripId);
     }

     Action enum safety: buttons map to exact backend enum strings only:
     - Accept → "liked"
     - Dismiss → "dismissed"
     - Save → "saved"
     - Converted → "converted_to_place"
     - Acted on → "acted_on"

     No other values. Define as a Dart enum with .value getter matching backend
     strings.

     Each method: try/catch DioException, log errors, rethrow as typed
     AdvisoryException for UI layer.

     B2. Riverpod Providers

     flutter/lib/features/advisory/providers/advisory_providers.dart

     // Repository singleton
     final advisoryRepositoryProvider = Provider<AdvisoryRepository>((ref) =>
     ...);

     // Trip-scoped advisory state (typed, not Map<String, dynamic>)
     final advisoryInsightsProvider =
     FutureProvider.family<List<AdvisoryInsight>, String>((ref, tripId) => ...);
     final advisoryBrainStateProvider =
     FutureProvider.family<AdvisoryBrainState, String>((ref, tripId) => ...);
     final advisoryJobsProvider = FutureProvider.family<List<AdvisoryJob>,
     String>((ref, tripId) => ...);

     // Action notifier (record accept/dismiss/save)
     final advisoryActionNotifierProvider =
     StateNotifierProvider.family<AdvisoryActionNotifier, AsyncValue<void>,
     String>(...);

     B3. Typed AdvisoryBrainState Model

     flutter/lib/features/advisory/data/models/advisory_brain_state.dart

     Proper data class instead of Map<String, dynamic>:
     class AdvisoryBrainState {
       final String lifecycleState;
       final String tripClass;
       final int cadenceSeconds;
       final String mode;
       final DateTime? lastCycleAt;
       final DateTime? nextEligibleAt;
       final DateTime? lastSeedAt;
       final String? lastSeedReason;
       final int ignoreStreak;
       final int advisedLocalitiesCount;
       final int advisedPoisCount;
       final List<String> pendingReseedReasons;
       final String? pausedReason;
       final DateTime? pausedAt;
       final int brightdataCallCount;

       factory AdvisoryBrainState.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) => ...;
     }

     B4. Feature Flag + Central Guard

     flutter/lib/core/config/feature_flags.dart

     Add:
     static bool get enableAdvisoryPipeline => _flag('ADVISORY_PIPELINE',
     defaultValue: false);

     flutter/lib/features/advisory/advisory_guard.dart — single guard function
     used by all advisory entry points:
     bool advisoryEnabled(WidgetRef ref) {
       return FeatureFlags.enableAdvisoryPipeline;
     }

     // Used in: editor overflow menu, live capture FAB, notification routing
     // One place to gate — no scattered flag checks

     Gate all advisory UI and background wiring behind this flag. Enables
     testing without affecting existing users.

     B4. Notification Routing

     flutter/lib/core/notifications/live_tracking_deep_link_bootstrap.dart

     Extend the notification handler to inspect the type field first (type =
     notification domain), then use action for navigation intent within that
     domain:

     // Current: assumes all payloads are trip-related
     // New: branch on type first (discriminator), then action (navigation 
     intent)
     final type = data['type'] as String?;
     final action = data['action'] as String?;

     if (!advisoryEnabled()) {
       // Feature flag off — fall through to existing routing
       _handleTripNotification(data);
       return;
     }

     switch (type) {
       case 'advisory':
         // action='open_inbox' → navigate to advisory debug/inbox for this trip
         _navigateToAdvisoryInbox(data['trip_id'], data['advisory_id']);
         break;
       case 'advisory_paused':
         // action='resume_advisory' → navigate to advisory resume prompt
         _navigateToAdvisoryResume(data['trip_id']);
         break;
       default:
         // Existing trip-based routing (checkin_candidate, etc.)
         _handleTripNotification(data);
     }

     Note: backend sends BOTH type and action in push data. type is the domain
     discriminator (advisory vs checkin_candidate); action is the deep-link
     intent (open_inbox, resume_advisory). We route on type first — this is the
     standard FCM pattern.

     B5. API Bridge Update

     flutter/lib/core/network/live_tracking_api.dart

     The generated AdvisoryApi class should be sufficient for all advisory
     calls. However, if the manual bridge pattern is used elsewhere for
     auth-header injection or retry logic, add a thin AdvisoryApiClient wrapper
     that mirrors the existing LiveTrackingApiClient pattern — constructing the
     AdvisoryApi instance with the same Dio client + auth interceptor.

     Check how the existing manual bridge creates its Dio instance and reuse
     that factory.

     Step C: Minimal Test UI (Advisory Debug Screen)

     Purpose

     Exercise the full backend round-trip without building the final UI.
     Accessible from the trip editor overflow menu (or a debug FAB) when
     enableAdvisoryPipeline is true.

     Screen: AdvisoryDebugScreen

     flutter/lib/features/advisory/presentation/screens/advisory_debug_screen.da
     rt

     Layout (simple ListView, not the final sheet-based UI):

     ┌──────────────────────────────────┐
     │ Advisory Debug — Trip: {title}   │
     ├──────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Brain State                      │
     │  lifecycle: active               │
     │  trip_class: long_road           │
     │  cadence: 3600s                  │
     │  mode: route                     │
     │  ignore_streak: 0                │
     │  localities covered: 3           │
     │  pois delivered: 7               │
     │  next_eligible_at: 14:32         │
     │  [Refresh] [Pause] [Resume]     │
     ├──────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Quick Actions                    │
     │  [Start Pre-Trip Advisory]       │
     ├──────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Insights (inbox)                 │
     │ ┌────────────────────────────┐   │
     │ │ 🍽️ Spice Garden (food_tip)│   │
     │ │ Great vegetarian thali...  │   │
     │ │ confidence: 0.82           │   │
     │ │ [Accept] [Dismiss] [Save]  │   │
     │ └────────────────────────────┘   │
     │ ┌────────────────────────────┐   │
     │ │ ⚠️ Scam alert (scam_alert)│   │
     │ │ Avoid tuk-tuk drivers...   │   │
     │ │ confidence: 0.71           │   │
     │ │ [Accept] [Dismiss] [Save]  │   │
     │ └────────────────────────────┘   │
     │ ... (scrollable)                 │
     ├──────────────────────────────────┤
     │ Jobs                             │
     │  pre_trip — completed — 2 min ago│
     │  location_trigger — processing   │
     │  ... (scrollable)                │
     └──────────────────────────────────┘

     Widgets

     - AdvisoryBrainStateCard — displays brain state fields,
     refresh/pause/resume buttons
     - AdvisoryInsightCard — displays one insight with accept/dismiss/save
     action buttons, category emoji
     - AdvisoryJobCard — displays job status + type
     - AdvisoryQuickActions — start pre-trip, force reseed buttons

     Behavior

     - On load: fetch brain state + insights + jobs in parallel
     - Accept button: POST /advisory/{id}/action with action=liked → refresh
     insights
     - Dismiss button: POST /advisory/{id}/action with action=dismissed →
     refresh insights
     - Save button: POST /advisory/{id}/action with action=saved → refresh
     insights
     - Pause button: POST /advisory/pause → refresh brain state
     - Resume button: POST /advisory/resume → refresh brain state
     - Start Pre-Trip: POST /advisory/start with job_type=pre_trip → refresh
     jobs (this effectively reseeds — no separate reseed button needed since the
      backend never implemented {"reseed": true} on the start schema)
     - Auto-refresh insights every 30s while screen is visible (timer)

     Entry Point

     - Trip editor screen overflow menu: add "Advisory Debug" item (guarded by
     enableAdvisoryPipeline)
     - OR: floating debug FAB on live capture screen (same guard)

✻ Crunched for 19m 49s

❯ hey, what is this                                                             
  Critical issue introduced                                                     
                                                                                
  Likely route payload regression in route_repository after generated type      
  change:                                                                       
  routeGeojson now maps only map values and sets scalar keys to null (type,     
  coordinates risk): route_repository.dart:519.                                 
  This can break route create/update request GeoJSON shape.
● Real bug. Let me trace it.
  first describe and analyze
  What _resolveRouteGeoJson(local) returns:
  {
    "type": "LineString",
    "coordinates": [[73.85, 18.52], [73.90, 18.55]]
  }

  What my conversion does (line 519):
  .map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, v is Map ? JsonObject(v) : null))

  - "type" → value is "LineString" (a String, not a Map) → maps to null
  - "coordinates" → value is [[73.85, 18.52], ...] (a List, not a Map) → maps to
   null

  Result: the backend receives {"type": null, "coordinates": null} — completely
  broken GeoJSON. Route create and update would silently send garbage. The same
  bug exists on line 598 (update path).

  Root cause: I assumed only Map values needed wrapping in JsonObject, but
  JsonObject is a built_value wrapper for any JSON value — strings, lists,
  numbers, booleans, maps, all of them. The v is Map check was wrong.

  Fix: wrap every non-null value in JsonObject(v), not just Maps:

  .map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, v != null ? JsonObject(v) : null))

  JsonObject("LineString") correctly wraps a string. JsonObject([[73.85, 
  18.52]]) correctly wraps a list. That's what built_value expects for
  BuiltMap<String, JsonObject?>.

  Want me to fix it now?

❯ Your analysis is correct.                                                     
                                                                                
  - `_resolveRouteGeoJson(local)` returns valid GeoJSON with scalar + list      
  values.                                                                       
  - Your current conversion:                                                    
    ```dart                                                                     
    .map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, v is Map ? JsonObject(v) : null))                
    ```                                                                         
    drops any non-Map JSON value.                                               
  - So:                                                                         
    - `type: "LineString"` becomes `null`                                       
    - `coordinates: [...]` becomes `null`                                       
  - Result is invalid payload (`{"type": null, "coordinates": null}`), so route 
   create/update sends broken GeoJSON.                                          
                                                                                
  Root cause:                                                                   
  - `JsonObject` is a wrapper for **any JSON-compatible value**, not just maps. 
  - The `v is Map` condition is the bug.                                        
                                                                                
  Correct fix:                                                                  
  ```dart                                                                       
  .map((k, v) => MapEntry(k, v != null ? JsonObject(v) : null))                 
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Also apply same fix in both create and update paths (your line 519 and 598    
  equivalents).                                                                 
  After patch, verify by logging serialized request body once and checking      
  `type` and `coordinates` are present and non-null.

● What to test, step by step

  ---
  Prerequisites (one-time setup)

  Backend .env — add these two lines if not already present:
  EXPOSE_ADVISORY_STATE_ENDPOINT=true
  OPENROUTER_API_KEY=sk-or-v1-...       # your existing key

  Flutter .env — add:
  ADVISORY_PIPELINE=true

  ---
  Part 1: Backend — confirm everything's clean

  Run from backend/:

  # 1. Verify migration is applied
  venv/Scripts/python -m alembic current
  # Expected: 9613649fe258 (head) (mergepoint)

  # 2. Run the full advisory test suite
  venv/Scripts/python -m pytest tests/test_trip_classifier.py
  tests/test_route_sampler.py tests/test_trip_brain_service.py
  tests/test_advisory_cycle_worker.py tests/test_advisory_ignore_sweep_worker.py
   tests/test_advisory_lifecycle_hooks.py tests/test_advisory_resilience.py -v
  # Expected: 40 passed

  # 3. Start the backend server
  venv/Scripts/python -m uvicorn app.main:app --reload --port 8000

  Track: all 40 tests pass, server starts without import errors.

  ---
  Part 2: Backend — start the 3 workers (separate terminals each)

  # Terminal 2: advisory worker (processes jobs)
  cd backend
  venv/Scripts/python -m app.workers.advisory_worker

  # Terminal 3: cycle worker (fires periodic location_trigger jobs)
  cd backend
  venv/Scripts/python -m app.workers.advisory_cycle_worker

  # Terminal 4: ignore sweep (detects unacted advisories)
  cd backend
  venv/Scripts/python -m app.workers.advisory_ignore_sweep_worker

  Track: each worker prints a startup log line. No crash on boot.

  ---
  Part 3: Flutter — build and run

  cd flutter
  flutter run --dart-define-from-file=.env

  Track: app compiles with zero errors, launches on device/emulator.

  ---
  Part 4: End-to-end flow — what to do in the app

  Step 1 — Create a trip:
  - Create a new trip with a title (e.g. "Pune to Chandigarh")
  - Add at least 2 places (start city + end city)
  - Optionally add a route between them

  What to check in the DB:
  SELECT trip_id, lifecycle_state, trip_class, mode, cadence_seconds,
  last_seed_at
  FROM trip_advisory_state
  ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;
  Expected: row exists, lifecycle_state = 'seeded', trip_class populated (e.g.
  long_road or intra_city), last_seed_at set.

  ---
  Step 2 — Open Advisory Debug screen:
  - Open the trip in the editor
  - Tap the ⋮ overflow menu (top right)
  - Tap "Advisory Debug"

  What you see:
  - Brain State card: lifecycle = seeded, trip_class, cadence, mode
  - Insights: empty ("No insights yet")
  - Jobs: empty ("No jobs yet")

  If brain state shows an error: check that EXPOSE_ADVISORY_STATE_ENDPOINT=true
  is in backend .env and the server was restarted after adding it.

  ---
  Step 3 — Start a pre-trip advisory:
  - In the debug screen, tap "Start Pre-Trip Advisory"
  - You should see a snackbar: "Pre-trip advisory job queued"

  What to track in Terminal 2 (advisory_worker):
  [ADVISORY_WORKER] claimed job_id=... trip_id=... type=pre_trip
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] route_segmentation ...
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] reddit_scrape ...          ← this takes 1-3 min (Crawl4AI +
  OpenRouter)
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] tripadvisor_scrape ... (stub — skipping)
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] gmaps_scrape ... (skipped — non-cycle job)
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] llm_extraction ...
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] scoring ...
  [ADVISORY_STAGE] delivery ...
  [ADVISORY_WORKER] completed job_id=...

  If reddit_scrape fails: check OPENROUTER_API_KEY is set and valid. The worker
  will retry up to 3 times with backoff.

  ---
  Step 4 — See insights appear:
  - Wait for the job to complete (~1-3 min)
  - In the debug screen, tap the refresh button (top right) or wait 30s for
  auto-refresh
  - Insights should now appear with category emojis, confidence scores, and

● Found it. The Flutter app is local-first — widget.tripId is a local Drift
  UUID, not the server PostgreSQL UUID. The backend has never seen that ID, so
  everything returns 404.

  The fix pattern already exists in the codebase:
  trip_repository.ensureRemoteTripId(localTripId) resolves local → server ID
  (creating the backend trip if needed). Route sync and place sync already use
  it. Advisory doesn't.

  Two things to fix:

  1. Advisory providers need to resolve the server trip ID before any API call
  2. Advisory debug screen needs access to TripRepository to call
  ensureRemoteTripId

  Searched for 2 patterns (ctrl+o to expand)

● Good — tripRepositoryProvider exists and gives access to ensureRemoteTripId.
  The cleanest fix: add a server-trip-ID resolution provider that advisory
  providers depend on, so every advisory API call uses the server ID
  automatically.

   have all advisory providers depend on it.

● Fixed as dac1070. Here's what changed and why it should work now:

  The bug: Flutter is local-first. Every widget.tripId in the editor/live
  screens is a local Drift UUID (e.g. a1b2c3d4-...). The backend has never seen
  that ID — its trips table uses different PostgreSQL UUIDs assigned when the
  trip is synced via POST /api/v1/trips. So every advisory API call was hitting
  404.

  The fix: Added serverTripIdProvider which calls the existing
  tripRepository.ensureRemoteTripId(localId) — the same resolver that route sync
   and place sync already use. It:
  1. Looks up the local trip's serverTripId field in Drift
  2. If null (not synced yet), calls POST /api/v1/trips to create it on the
  backend
  3. Returns the server UUID
  4. Caches it so subsequent calls in the same session don't re-fetch

  Every advisory provider and debug screen action now resolves through this
  before hitting the API.

  To re-test: hot-restart the Flutter app, open a trip → overflow menu →
  Advisory Debug. The brain state card should load (showing seeded or the state
  from the trip creation hook). "Start Pre-Trip Advisory" should return 202
  instead of 404.
